function AttackMultipleTargets(u, ...)
	if not RegisterHit or not RegisterAttack then
		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		if not u or #u == 705355079 % 5553977 then
			return;
		end;
		local y = {};
		for u, V in pairs(u) do
			local Z = V:FindFirstChild(cB(97118 - 141194));
			if Z then
				table[cB(-224243 - (-180023))](y, { V, Z });
			end;
		end;
		if #y == 1851260016 % 16382832 then
			return;
		end;
		RegisterAttack:FireServer(-80468 + 80468);
		RegisterHit:FireServer(y[2668744321 % 15698496][937862 + -937860], y);
	end);
end;
function StartFastAttack(...)
	if FastAttackRunning then
		return;
	end;
	FastAttackRunning = true;
	spawn(function(...)
		while FastAttackEnabled do
			RunService[cB(500972 - 545116)]:Wait();
			local u = player[cB(-129894 + 86036)];
			local y = u and u:FindFirstChild(cB(496201 - 539790));
			if y then
				local u = {};
				for V, Z in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if Z ~= player and (Z[cB(-886502 - (-842644))] and not BlacklistedPlayers[Z[cB(422040 + -465987)]]) then
						local V = Z[cB(52639 - 96497)]:FindFirstChild(cB(-948103 + 904432));
						local x = Z[cB(-771836 - (-727978))]:FindFirstChild(cB(757044 + -800633));
						if V and (x and (V[cB(-712987 + 669443)] > 1444229655 % 8752907 and ((x[cB(-738864 - (-695170))] - y[cB(-648514 - (-604820))]))[cB(740787 - 784650)] <= FastAttackRange)) then
							table[cB(-648907 - (-604687))](u, Z[cB(495966 + -539824)]);
						end;
					end;
				end;
				local V = workspace:FindFirstChild(cB(-1071568 - (-1027462)));
				if V then
					for V, Z in pairs(V:GetChildren()) do
						local x = Z:FindFirstChild(cB(-93461 + 49790));
						local q = Z:FindFirstChild(cB(168569 + -212158));
						if x and (q and (x[cB(439912 + -483456)] > 109549880 % 1991816 and ((q[cB(504644 + -548338)] - y[cB(608269 + -651963)]))[cB(-420582 - (-376719))] <= FastAttackRange)) then

						end;
					end;
				end;

					AttackMultipleTargets(u);
				end;

		end;

		return;

		end;
	end;

function ClearTracers(...)
	for u, y in pairs(TracersList) do
		pcall(function(...)
			if y[cB(-528393 + 484835)] then
				y:Remove();
			elseif y[cB(-944392 + 900180)] then
				y:Destroy();
			end;
		end);
	end;
	TracersList = {};
end;

	end;

		end);

function getClosestPlayer(u, ...)

		local u = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if u and (u[cB(833025 - 876883)] and u[cB(-9373 - 34485)]:FindFirstChild(cB(924576 + -968165))) then
			local y = u[cB(-700686 + 656828)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(cB(766545 + -810216));
			if y and y[cB(-637561 + 594017)] > 628841988 % 5822611 then
				return u[cB(978471 - 1022329)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local Z, x = nil, math[cB(124926 - 169152)];
	for u, q in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if q ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[q[cB(397577 - 441524)]] and (q[cB(185221 + -229079)] and q[cB(655283 + -699141)]:FindFirstChild(cB(-105803 + 62214)))) then

			if u and u[cB(-543020 - (-499476))] > 140454 + -140454 then
				local u = ((q[cB(-781108 - (-737250))][cB(-977827 + 934238)][cB(-741902 + 698208)] - y[cB(511065 - 554759)]))[cB(706753 - 750616)];

	return Z;

function ClearAura(...)
	for u, y in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if y and y[cB(455847 - 499807)] then
			y:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
function ApplyAura(u, ...)
	ClearAura();
	if not u then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	local y = AURA_DEFS[u];
	if not y then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	ActiveAura = u;
	local V = player[cB(680213 - 724071)];
	if not V then
		return;
	end;
	local Z = V:FindFirstChild(cB(880662 - 924251));
	if not Z then
		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		local u = Instance[cB(-540701 - (-496473))](cB(-960375 - (-916453)));
		u[cB(-615028 + 571081)] = cB(-564429 + 520597);
		u[cB(712361 + -756511)] = cB(-748818 + 705211);
		u[cB(-207973 + 164028)] = y[cB(172102 - 216047)];
		u[cB(922153 - 965939)] = y[cB(-732057 - (-688271))];
		u[cB(-648352 + 604406)] = y[cB(50534 - 94480)];
		u[cB(661889 + -705514)] = y[cB(-175491 + 131866)];
		u[cB(446022 + -490131)] = y[cB(-343319 + 299210)];
		u[cB(-1076265 - (-1032274))] = y[cB(-226318 + 182327)];
		u[cB(-102062 - (-58521))] = y[cB(588472 - 632013)];
		u[cB(851943 - 895644)] = y[cB(-999750 - (-956049))];
		u[cB(964351 + -1008087)] = y[cB(399155 + -442891)];
		u[cB(-342058 - (-298098))] = Z;
		table[cB(-264023 - (-219803))](AuraObjects, u);
		local V = Instance[cB(-210723 + 166495)](cB(-136480 + 92450));
		V[cB(-568535 - (-524588))] = cB(230033 + -274064);
